Reading method: diff-based. For the 1,139 rebound rows the removed unit text (shownSourceText) was checked by script to be contained verbatim in its history snapshot (1,139/1,139 exact); for each file the removed units were grouped by what they are and read in full where they could be live (anchor sheets in the per-file scratch), judged against the restored current text and live consumers. The 35 newly retired rows were compared one by one with the restored unit that supersedes them; their old text is not contained in the target by design (supersede), the old version being kept in liveness-retired-unit-text.json.

- 1174 rows (1,139 rebound, 35 new): ok 1090, rework 76, hold 8 (rework+hold 7.2%).
- Newly retired rows with the restored unit not carrying the dropped live content are rework; see Note.
- The four unchanged inherited payload retirements were not re-reviewed (not edits).
